    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM (
        SELECT DISTINCT [d1].[UserId]
        FROM [Donations] AS [d1]
        WHERE [d].[Id] = [d1].[DonationCaseId]
    ) AS [t]) AS [DonorsCount], [d].[ImagePath]
FROM [DonationCases] AS [d]
WHERE [d].[Status] = 1
ORDER BY [d].[CreatedAt] DESC
